[downloads] Share the download item icon helper with the toolbar tray

Move the Downloads page's icon lookup to DownloadIconUtils in
chrome/browser/download/android so the download toolbar tray can use it
instead of its own copy. The page's DOCUMENT and NONE cases were
unreachable (Filters maps DOCUMENT to OTHER), so the shared switch keys
on OfflineItemFilter directly with no change in behavior, and the two
drawables only those cases referenced are removed.

[downloads] Open incognito media downloads in an incognito tab

On desktop form factors, media and web downloads open in a new tab via
MediaViewerUtils, which doesn't carry the download's profile, so
incognito downloads opened in a regular tab. Pass it through from both
the download open path and the notification open path, setting
EXTRA_OPEN_NEW_INCOGNITO_TAB as the PDF path already does, and drop the
tray's PDF-only workaround.

Highlight blocks when selecting PDF translated text in Reading Mode

When translated content is selected in Reading Mode, whole blocks are
synced to the main panel. This change uses the CSS Custom Highlight API
to visually highlight the corresponding whole blocks in the side panel
so users can see what is selected in the main panel without modifying
the user's actual text selection.

The block highlight is displayed only in the side panel and is cleared
when the selection collapses, when Read Aloud plays from the selection,
or when a selection update from the main panel is applied. It is
maintained across presentation state, but hidden in immersive mode.

https://drive.google.com/file/d/1wtTfstRxCyZNjkYukGzYlOHyZoSacGcw/view?usp=sharing

[ReaderMode] Deflake and re-enable DistillabilityServiceTest

testServiceAliveAfterNativePage failed 100% of runs (3200/3200 on LUCI
reviver) because it polled MessageDispatcher synchronously right after
loadUrl() for MessageIdentifier.READER_MODE, which races with
DistillabilityAgent::DidMeaningfulLayout and is only enqueued on
CustomTabs.

Observe TabDistillabilityProvider via DistillabilityObserver instead and
remove @DisabledTest (0/1 failures on emulator after fix).

Remove expired CrOSEnforceSystemAec* flags and dead code

- Remove the expired `enforce-system-aec`, `enforce-system-aec-agc`,
`enforce-system-aec-ns`, and `enforce-system-aec-ns-agc` flags, their
`base::Feature` definitions, and field trial testing configs.
- Inline the enabled-by-default `kCrOSEnforceSystemAec` state and
disabled-by-default NS/AGC enforcement states in `AudioManagerCras`.
- Remove the now-unreachable `kCrOSSystemAEC` feature,
`--system-aec-enabled` switch, `EnforceSystemAudioEchoCancellation()`
browser client plumbing, `kDeviceSystemAecEnabled` CrosSetting, and
unreleased `DeviceSystemAecEnabled` policy.

[Actor] Display task step progress in Peek View subtitle

While a task runs, the Peek View subtitle only says it is working, so
users must open the notification to see what it is doing. This shows
the notification's step progress in the Peek View subtitle and its
TalkBack content description.

- Adds DESCRIPTION_TEXT to TabBottomSheetPeekProperties.
  TabBottomSheetPeekViewBinder shows it when non-empty and falls back to
  DESCRIPTION_TEXT_ID otherwise.
- ActorControlCoordinator reads the step from
  ActorTask#getCurrentActionName(), as the notification does, in ACTING
  and REFLECTING. Native only changes the step when glic sends a new
  one, so it stays visible through REFLECTING.
- ActorControlStateTracker refreshes the UI from
  onTaskStepProgressUpdated for the tracked running task without
  storing a copy of the step.
- ActorControlMediator adds the step to the content description.
- Like the notification, this is gated by ActorStepProgressNotification.

Screen recording: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1JHKtVfVxj3CzEP07V3dkNaZbJN6uEg-i/view?usp=sharing

44ecb286ca798836cf06d6b5d9e5db61d236d3a9 [ diff ] 
Revert "Improve native occlusion tracking on Windows." and dependent CL

This reverts two commits:
* 49af5f7e9dc5878968cfe82152d91d90753c8fc7 ("Improve native occlusion
  tracking on Windows.", crrev.com/c/8469511)
* 1775ec03a1389c3b2a0f3d3fba3dbe7d5b9f0632 ("Use WindowTracker in
  NativeWindowOcclusionTrackerWin", crrev.com/c/8560011)

Reason for revert: the first CL causes https://crbug.com/582407452 in
the Windows native file picker. The native file dialog is a WS_POPUP
window, which that CL started treating as an occluder, so a dialog
covering the browser window makes the WebContents OCCLUDED and the File
System Access picker gets cancelled with AbortError. The second CL
builds on the first CL's multiple-windows-per-HWND model (including
OnMinimizeEnd() and its tests), so it can't stay without the first CL
and is reverted too.

Later unrelated changes to the same files are kept: crrev.com/c/8509062
(location change filtering), crrev.com/c/8680280 (DCHECK to CHECK), and
crrev.com/c/8670706 (RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION). The location change filter
unit tests now pass base::DoNothing() as the occlusion state callback,
since the WeakPtr tracker parameter they used is reverted.

Original change's description (crrev.com/c/8469511):
> Improve native occlusion tracking on Windows.
>
> * Handle cases where an Aura Window's HWND is not the root HWND, which
> can occur when embedding Chromium in another app.
>
> * When removing event hooks due to all windows being minimized, leave
> the MINIMIZEEND hook. When a window is unminimized, handle it like a
> visibility change. For Aura windows whose HWND is the root, this already

> windows.

> with "tooltip" in their classname, since other things are less likely to
> be transient.

parent
4a7f81896ba309d4d2eaec0e7b5616cd1c34be04 [ diff ] 
[Mac] Forward Cmd+KeyUp events to key window in app shims

https://crrev.com/c/7807835 worked around AppKit swallowing KeyUp events
when the Command modifier is held by manually forwarding them to the key
window in -[BrowserCrApplication sendEvent:]. However, windowed PWAs on
macOS run out-of-process in app shims using AppShimApplication, which
did not override -sendEvent: and therefore still dropped Cmd+KeyUp
events.

Move the shared -sendEvent: event-dispatch logic (including Cmd+KeyUp
forwarding, JIS Eisu/Kana KeyDown forwarding, ScopedSendingEvent, and
the "nsevent" crash key) from BrowserCrApplication into the shared
CrApplication base class so both Chrome windows and PWA app shims
receive Cmd+KeyUp events.

Also guard the feature check on self.keyWindow being non-nil so key
releases during app shim bootstrap (before FinalizeFeatureState() runs
and before any windows exist) cannot trigger an early FeatureList access
crash.

fd1544d6dec757edd1065acfd664160a5ade4da1 [ diff ] 
android: Run lint as a native image

## wnwen (human)
This migrates JVM lint to GraalVM lint on x64 linux.

## Agent generated details
Today the lint step of chrome_public_apk is one lint run on the JVM:
151.7 s and 607 s of CPU on a 48-core machine.

A lint process spends much of that loading lint's classes and
JIT-compiling them. The lint-graalvm native image (built by
//third_party/android_build_tools/lint/3pp since crrev.com/c/8686692,
in the lint CIPD package that DEPS pins) is compiled ahead of time, so
it has neither cost.

lint.py now runs that binary instead of the JVM on linux-x64 for
targets that use the default lint jar, with -Xmx and
-XX:ActiveProcessorCount=2 (which sizes G1's threads for 2 CPUs). There
is no GN arg. Other hosts (macOS, linux-arm64) and targets with a custom
lint_jar_path, such as Clank's chrome_apk, keep the JVM. Like javac and
Error Prone, the native image is always an input (remote actions run on
linux-x64), and lint.jar and the JDK only when the host is not
linux-x64.

The image also needs the JDK's AWT libraries (libawt.so and 7 others)
that native-image writes next to it, since lint reaches java.awt and
javax.imageio; the 3pp copies them (copy_shared_libs).

Re-running the lint action of chrome_public_apk__lint with lint
32.6.0-alpha03 (48-core machine, autoninja wall time, CPU is user +
sys, mean of 4 JVM and 3 native runs):

  config   wall      CPU     lint RSS   issues
  JVM      151.7 s   607 s   5.3 GB     186
  native   126.4 s   223 s   4.5 GB     186 (same)

Issues were compared without the baseline: both report the same 13
errors and 173 warnings.

Per-library lint analysis (crrev.com/c/8667642) gains more, since it
runs 692 short lint processes: 64.1 s native against 249.1 s on JVMs.

[Wayland] Treat text-input-v1 delete index as relative to the cursor

tree
22f24223579695f2e7e7429e25681a7783f0d25f
parent
9ed3227cec5a211948050cc10d4d9c71b19ffabf [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Treat text-input-v1 delete index as relative to the cursor

text-input-v1 specifies the delete_surrounding_text index as a byte
offset relative to the current cursor position. Chrome treated it as an
absolute offset into the surrounding text, a workaround for exo
(crbug.com/40189286). As a result, the common request from input
methods, a negative index such as delete_surrounding_text(-1, 1) to
delete the character before the cursor, was rejected with "The
selection range for surrounding text is invalid.", and the character
stayed in place. Vietnamese Telex input through IBus on Weston produced
"Vieêệt" instead of "Việt".

Interpret the index relative to the cursor of the tracked surrounding
text, and ignore invalid deletion requests from the compositor with
LOG(ERROR) instead of hitting a DFATAL. text-input-v3 keeps passing
absolute offsets.

Also send the end of the selection as the cursor and its start as the
anchor in set_surrounding_text, matching gfx::Range and the deletion
code. Previously the two were swapped, so for a non-empty selection the
input method computed relative indices from the wrong end.

[X11] Process key events from every master keyboard
📋 Metadata
commit
daacc045502047fd60cbaefb416bb095fea1d3bb
[ log ]
author

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 19:22:22 2026
tree
85531897851052e4c1d1f72f1c9d828c97b5a913
parent
32f99a1f651d97f1b5dac5ea4d50e2730c1019de [ diff ] 
[X11] Process key events from every master keyboard

With MPX (e.g. after `xinput create-master`), the X server has more
than one master keyboard. TouchFactory stored a single "virtual core
keyboard" ID, which each MasterKeyboard in the device list overwrote,
so the newly created master won and key events from the Virtual core
keyboard (i.e. all physical keyboards) were dropped by
ShouldProcessDeviceEvent().

Track the set of master keyboards instead and accept key events from
any of them. Key events reported by slave keyboards are still ignored.

Tested on Xorg + openbox: after `xinput create-master test 0 1`,
typing via the Virtual core keyboard reaches a focused <input> again.

ca3375d1d2c3fff4b8b02ba0c5832e3a9a957b98 [ diff ] 
[🌷] Filter flaky a11y InputTextValueChanged test on A15 tablet

All/DumpAccessibilityTreeTest.AccessibilityInputTextValueChanged has
been 50-70% flaky on android-15-tablet-x64-rel since 2026-11-06 (66
flaky and 1 hard failure in about 100 runs), after zero flakes in the
prior week. It passes on all other Android CI builders.

The test changes input values on a 1000 ms timer, and the Android tree
on the tablet emulator now first sees the fields only after they have
changed. Filter it on this emulator config only while the owners make
the test deterministic.

Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fcontent%2Ftest%5C%3Acontent_browsertests%21gtest%3A%3ADumpAccessibilityTreeTest%23AccessibilityInputTextValueChanged%2FAll.android
Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-15-tablet-x64-rel/10886/overview

tree
b15c292e44c20394e9db2a90b209843a7d15d509
parent
82997aeb768426112bd55aab43317d5430d57d71 [ diff ] 
enterprise_companion: stop the test server before checking expectations

TestServer::~TestServer() iterated `request_matcher_groups_` while the
embedded test server was still running. The server only shut down when
`test_server_handle_` was destroyed, after the destructor body, and
`HandleRequest`, which is bound with base::Unretained(this) and runs on
the server's IO thread, could race with the checks or touch members
that had already been destroyed.

Shut the server down at the start of the destructor, as the updater's
ScopedServer does.

parent
d2f429656be03ff90507086d2e4b487105e2115b [ diff ] 
Deflake BrowserNavigatorTestAlwaysOnTop resize restrictions test

AlwaysOnTopPopupMoveAndResizeRestrictions resolved its promise on the
first 'resize' event after window.resizeTo()/resizeBy(). On Wayland the
window manager can apply the resize in several steps, so that event may
report an intermediate window.outerWidth (e.g. 316 instead of 340), and
the following resizeBy() then starts from the stale width.

Poll window.outerWidth until it reaches the requested width instead.

Flake rates (--gtest_repeat, --test-launcher-retry-limit=0):
- linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi (UTR, chromium:try): 10/20 failed before,
  0/40 after.
- Local x64 mutter (--use-mutter --ozone-platform=wayland, 8 jobs):
  7/40 failed before, 0/40 after.
Raising the UI test timeouts does not help (7/20 failed), as expected
for a value mismatch.

Fixed: 584550195, 584546922
Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi,linux-wayland-mutter-rel

tree
e5367ae50b8b1529e7892c66bd62f1356c793e7d
parent
2b622035871903cf2f42f1a6dbc016d6c2096546 [ diff ] 
[Cast Android] Synchronize SetPaused and FeedDataContinue in AudioSink

AudioSinkAndroidAudioTrackImpl::SetPaused() was executing on the caller
thread while FeedData() and FeedDataContinue() executed on the feeder
thread without synchronization on state_, pending_data_, or the pending
byte counters. An unpause racing with an in-flight writePcm() or a
Play->Pause->Play sequence could invoke FeedDataContinue() concurrently
or twice for the same buffer, causing out-of-bounds memmove() reads,
duplicate OnWritePcmCompletion() callbacks, or null dereferences.

Additionally, PreventDelegateCalls() did not invalidate in-flight
completion tasks already posted to caller_task_runner_ via weak_this_.

Fix by:
1. Routing SetPaused() to feeder_task_runner_ via RUN_ON_FEEDER_THREAD
   and only calling FeedDataContinue() when a reformatted buffer is
   partially fed (pending_data_ &&
   pending_data_bytes_after_reformat_ > 0).
2. Resetting pending_data_ and byte counters in PostPcmCallback(),
   SignalError(), and FinalizeOnFeederThread(), and guarding
   NotifyWritePcmCompletion() with weak_factory_.InvalidateWeakPtrs() in
   PreventDelegateCalls().

Bug: 583124371
Test: cast_android_cma_backend_unittests --gtest_filter=AudioSinkAndroidAudioTrackImplTest.*

Roll ds_store and mac_alias and add stub .dist-info metadata

Upstream ds_store and mac_alias switched to setuptools_scm and now call
importlib.metadata.version() at import time. Because generate_dsstore.py
imports both libraries directly from their DEPS source checkouts via
sys.path without wheel installation, importing them raised
PackageNotFoundError.

Add stub .dist-info/METADATA files in third_party/ds_store and
third_party/mac_alias and include those directories on sys.path in
generate_dsstore.py, and add unit test coverage in mac_signing_tests so
future rolls are exercised on CQ.

tree
63372e0aa0bc41c77372db1a78b65eabfa0411a8
parent
e70aa66abfc49cde25a3358d1028aeaabb3e8992 [ diff ] 
cast: Don't draw the default focus ring in Cast receiver apps

Cast receiver apps draw their own focus indication. When an app
focuses a full-screen element (e.g. the container of its D-pad
controls), the default focus ring shows up as a thin border around
the whole screen for as long as that element stays focused.

Insert ":focus { outline: none; }" as a user style sheet in every
document from CastContentRendererClient. This overrides only the
default outline; an outline-style set by the page still applies.
Pages that relied on the default ring, including ones that only set
outline-color or outline-offset, no longer get one. Other embedders
are unchanged.

Bug: b:582622041
Test: cast_shell_browsertests --gtest_filter=CastNavigationBrowserTest.NoDefaultFocusRing

tree
c28091c03778ce7852a530804fc23da4dad6f430
parent
cba5429882b3684cdd6e6bb09fc2d8592ad6da85 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Add ExecuteJs mojom types, result codes and limits

Add the IPC types for an upcoming ExecuteJs actor tool, which runs a
model-provided script in an isolated world in the target tab's main
frame and returns the result:

- ExecuteJsAction, sent to the renderer as a new ToolAction member.
- ExecuteJsResponse, returned to the browser in ActionResult.
- ActionResultCodes for a failed script, a timeout, a navigation while
  the script runs, and a target that isn't the main frame.
- Limits on the run time, the result size, and the logged script size.

Nothing sends or handles the new types yet.

Bug: b:582469127

d8d785163c4c1258c1c7487e3f7881925f8d43fe

author

Wed Nov 11 18:57:47 2026
tree
91c381f49c31e6a88a737c5d86bc10b4848246db
parent
754bab4940299a4f8b5bbdb0935632f21440ffed [ diff ] 
[remoting] Apply AV1 bitrate config before codec initialization

Calls `WebrtcVideoEncoderAV1::UpdateConfig()` before
`InitializeCodec()` on both initial codec creation and resolution
changes so `aom_codec_enc_init()` initializes its rate-control buffer
level with the actual target bitrate (including the per-megapixel
minimum from `SetFrameSize()`) rather than the 1000 kbps default
(`EncoderBitrateFilter::kDefaultMinimumBitrateKbps`), since
`av1_change_config()` only clamps `buffer_level` down and never scales
it up.

Also resets `codec_` alongside `image_` on resolution changes so
`UpdateConfig()` does not push the new configuration into the outgoing
codec instance via `aom_codec_enc_config_set()`, and so the old encoder
is freed before allocating the new one, lowering peak memory at high
resolutions.

[ChromeDriver] Honor independent Set Window Rect components

Requests containing only width, height, x or y currently return an
unchanged rectangle because both ChromeDriver layers require complete
component pairs. Preserve each supplied component independently through
CDP serialization and completion matching, keeping omitted components
absent during restoration.

Add native coverage for partial requests, validation, restoration and
state-only commands. Narrow the WPT failure expectation to Android while
retaining platform-specific subtest baselines.

parent
88f75363f1d7fde2d3cfba8a53394878a6235466 [ diff ] 
[Performance Manager] Stop idle commit-limit polling

The Windows urgent-discard policy observes the shared memory monitor for
its entire lifetime. After the last page disappears, that observation
keeps system memory polling alive with nothing to discard.

Observe only while the graph has pages, including pages without browser
windows. Reset observation on graph detachment and resume when page
demand returns, without affecting other observers or memory-coordinator
driven discarding.

[TTC][CAL][6/N] Distinguish tools acting on WebContents from navigation tools

Add ActsOnWebContents() to ToolRequest to distinguish tools that require
a live renderer from browser-level navigation and reload tools.

ExecutionEngine checks for a crashed renderer before tool invocation.
Tools that navigate or reload can recover or replace a crashed renderer.
ExecutionEngine now permits them to execute on crashed tabs.

Also check ActorTask presence at the ToolController entry point.
Assert task presence in ToolController::ReloadPage directly.

9b6ebdaec8ff51a02a35a3c8141bbca1e623712e
parent
2894ec1688e1d69780f5594551d64f95c900b059 [ diff ] 
[Fonts] Restrict PUA and notdef resolutions and caching

According to the CSS Fonts 4 spec [1] generic font families and
installed font fallback (last resort fonts) should not be used to match
Private-Use Area (PUA) codepoints. To enforce this both at initial
resolution time and at potential cache time, this change:

* Exposes through `FontFallbackList` (and part of `Font`) if a given
  (`Simple`)`FontData` came from a generic family or last resort (GFoLR)
* In `FontFallbackIterator`, restricts the range of returned
  `FontDataForRangeSet`s to exclude PUA if coming from a GFoLR
  * To protect against some potential work, this adds a reusable non-PUA
   `UnicodeRangeSet` to the `FontGlobalContext`
* Exposes `HasNotDefGlyph` on `ShapeResults`
* Update `NGShapeCache` to:
  * not cache any shape results that include `.notdef`, because
    different font fallback lists might provide a definition for that
    codepoint
  * not use the cache to get shape results that include PUA and come
    from a GFoLR, because a named resource might have populated them in
    the cache already

This change also re-enables the `font-unicode-PUA.html` WPT, which
validates this behavior end-to-end, and adds some new tests to cover
specific sub-portions.

[1] https://www.w3.org/TR/css-fonts-4/#char-handling-issues

9534039f85114893066f7c67e2eb9de7a8f9375c [ diff ] 
[crubit] Roll Crubit to support `__declspec(dllimport)` on Windows.

This CL rolls Crubit from 6cdbd6b427cb to 6ce4ac2e0763:
https://chromium.googlesource.com/external/github.com/google/crubit/+log/6cdbd6b427cb..6ce4ac2e0763

Notable changes:

* cacc72a2875e: Add the `CRUBIT_NEVER_BITWISE_RELOCATABLE` annotation.
  It marks a C++ type as never bitwise-relocatable, so Crubit never
  treats it (or types that contain it) as Rust-movable (`Unpin`).
* 6ce4ac2e0763: Accept `__declspec(dllimport)` and
  `__declspec(dllexport)` in `rs_bindings_from_cc`.  On Windows,
  `COMPONENT_EXPORT(...)` expands to these attributes, and before this
  change Crubit did not generate bindings for such C++ APIs (e.g. for
  `GURL`).

committer

Wed Nov 11 18:48:41 2026
tree
580eaa670c6205a03baffe306e590918eae19085
parent
da165ff8cdf487d519f431ecf1994887252330e4 [ diff ] 
[SkillsV2] Redo the webview handshake when the guest loads a new document

SkillsWebviewBridge only sent skills-handshake while it wasn't connected,
and it stayed connected after the first handshake. When Back or Forward
reloaded the webview through syncWebviewToPath(), the new Skills Library
document never got a handshake, so it had no way to message
chrome://skills and older web app versions stayed blank.

The webview fires loadstart only for cross-document loads, so treat a
top-level loadcommit that follows a loadstart as a new document and drop
the old connection. Route changes in the guest keep the connection, and
so do loads that abort or never commit, like mailto: links.

f672822c2c00f4f952125c82119abcee7322cb98 [ diff ] 
[iOS][ODE] Disable kIOSPasskeyOnDeviceEncryptionStateTracker by default

PasskeyTrustedVaultOnDeviceEncryptionStateTracker synchronously calls
IOSTrustedVaultClient::FetchKeys() during profile service creation,
which crashes on cold start when a persisted primary account has been
removed from the shared iOS SSO keychain.

Disable the kill switch by default so it can be merged to M159 while
fixing the underlying missing-identity handling on main. Password ODE
metrics remain unaffected.

[media] Remove AllowDelayedAudioFocusGainAndroid feature flag

AllowDelayedAudioFocusGainAndroid has been enabled by default since
M150 (crrev.com/c/7920288), and its chrome://flags entry reaches its
expiry milestone in M160.

Remove the feature, its chrome://flags entry and the Java constant.
AudioFocusDelegate now always accepts a delayed focus gain and treats
AUDIOFOCUS_REQUEST_DELAYED as success.

e25b0ed17cd9f7cf7aafc2362b450ca221d886c0
parent
e7c1502f8621180d0bb3ecec7048ce35181cc70d [ diff ] 
[Read Aloud] Remove right-arrow preview instruction from voice dialog labels

In voice-selection-dialog, each cr-radio-button previously wrapped its
display name in readingModeLanguageMenuItemLabel (", use right arrow key
to preview voice"), which was inherited from the cr-action-menu
dropdown. Unlike cr-action-menu (role="application"), cr-dialog is a
standard dialog where screen readers intercept ArrowRight for
text/cursor navigation and each voice row already provides its own
preview button.

Set cr-radio-button's label directly to voiceDisplayName_(voiceItem) and
remove the unused voiceLabel_() helper so screen readers announce the
voice name, radio state/position, and voice description without a
non-functional shortcut instruction.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/1iln8j2k36vq8

tree
bc80ec38d7f530b0f42b21c1f9d7a2a3b3c3390d
parent
9f5ed62f6f3e098b320944f4d479c3b2e8702adb [ diff ] 
reporting: Do not depend on User or policy state on user removal

UserAddedRemovedReporter (and the cache in DeviceCloudPolicyManagerAsh)
looked up the User via UserManager in OnUserToBeRemoved() to filter
out guest and device local accounts, and checked the reporting policy
at the time of notification.

This is a preparation to defer the user removal for logged in users
to the next start up. In that case, the removal can be processed
while the User instance is not available, and before the device
settings become trusted.

So:
- Determine whether the removal is a reporting target by AccountId.
- Check the reporting policy when the event is actually reported,
  waiting for the device settings to be trusted.

Bug: 583613638
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='UserAddedRemovedReporterTest.*'
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter='*UserAddedRemovedReporter*'

703724fd611f0bf8ead1bc5e8b0955d7bc1fc748 [ diff ] 
[context-input] Enforce 20 MiB file size limit on image uploads

Enforces a 20 MiB (20 * 1024 * 1024 bytes) default file size limit on
image uploads across Composebox entrypoints (NTP, Omnibox popup, and
Contextual Searchbox handler), while keeping the existing 100 MiB limit
for PDF and other file attachments.

Provides a Finch feature flag (`ComposeboxImageFileSizeLimit`, enabled
by default) and parameter fallback (`limit_all_images`, default `true`)
that, when set to `false`, restricts only raw `image/heic` and
`image/heif` uploads to 20 MiB while allowing client-downscaled images
up to the 100 MiB general attachment limit.

NO_IFTTT=Shared logic placed in ComposeboxEmbedderMixin.

tree
751fb9a3bd39eb47c45672c5b009045e5f5b8799
parent
ec773fb057c83b38ff4ae276e7eeef513fc62c31 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Fix UrlBar crash on Shift+touch without a selection anchor

ArrowKeyMovementMethod extends a Shift+touch selection on ACTION_MOVE
and ACTION_UP from a private anchor that it records on ACTION_DOWN.
When that ACTION_DOWN never reached the movement method (e.g. TextView
had no layout yet) or Shift was pressed mid-gesture, the framework
calls Selection.setSelection(buffer, -1, offset), which throws an
IndexOutOfBoundsException from UrlBar.onTouchEvent. This is an Android
bug (b/190156873) that also reproduces on a stock EditText.

The anchor is not reachable from outside the framework, so drop the
Shift/selecting meta state before each touch event reaches TextView.
The movement method then never takes its selecting path on touch.

Two things change for hardware keyboards as a result: Shift + finger
drag moves the cursor instead of selecting, and if Shift is held
through a touch, Shift+Arrow moves instead of extending until Shift is
pressed again. Taps, Shift+click, mouse-drag selection and Shift+Arrow
without a touch in between are unchanged.

tree
d3e3bd6583610a315a0cd2422f17f7f07f87940b
parent
5ce124446a71c4d78a63b87d0721620e8628506a [ diff ] 
[FedCM] Move IdP links from the config file to the well-known file

crrev.com/c/8680223 parses the IdP's account management page, privacy
policy and terms of service links from its config file. This CL parses
them from its well-known file instead, with the same keys, and no longer
reads them from the config file, so all configs on the IdP's site share
one set of links. These keys aren't part of the FedCM spec.

Only absolute HTTP(S) URLs are accepted, like the RP's privacy policy
and terms of service URLs from the client metadata endpoint. Relative
URLs would resolve against the well-known file's URL, which can be on
the web-identity.well-known subdomain that only serves that file, so
they would likely point to pages that don't exist.

A link that isn't an absolute HTTP(S) URL same-site with the config URL
is ignored with a console warning that names the well-known file. As
before, the warning is logged even if the IdP fails validation later.
IdPs that skip the well-known check (same-site with the RP, registered,
or with FedCmWithoutWellKnownEnforcement enabled) only have links if
they still serve a well-known file, but it doesn't need a valid
provider_urls list.

The links are still only shown behind kFedCmIdpLinks, whose
chrome://flags description now names the well-known file.

[omnibox][webUI] Respect modifier keys on Enter on keyword chip

crrev.com/c/8752441 enabled pressing Enter on a focused keyword chip to
navigate. But it left handling modifier keys / disposition as a todo to
keep the CL small.

This CL adds a new mojom method for webUI to call `OpenPopupSelection()`
with keyboard modifiers to allow the cc side to calculate and use the
correct disposition.

It renames the existing `OpenPopupSelection()` to
`OpenPopupSelectionWithDisposition()` to support existing webUI
callsites.

This matches how opening other matches work through
`OpenAutocompleteMatch()` via keyboard modifiers, not disposition.

I'll look into another followup CL to migrate the existing ctrl+enter
match navigations to likewise pass in modifier keys instead of
disposition.

parent
142ff2e209453cfe27601352665e0b59a187617b [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Wire Vertical Tabs and GTS to a grouped provider

Vertical Tabs and the Grid Tab Switcher still build their lists straight
from the tab model. With this change, each one gets its list from a
GroupedTabListDataProvider when its layout is enabled under
TabUiDataVirtualization, so they move to the same push-based path as the
group dialog, tab strip and editor. Vertical Tabs lets groups expand to
show their tabs; GTS keeps groups as single cards.

Both stay off by default. GTS is also gated on the Android Tab UI
refactor, and it needs a few more fixes before it can be turned on: some
drag-and-drop state in the grid is tracked by list position, and the
other grouped surfaces (like the tab selection editor) still read from
the tab model directly. For example, dragging one tab onto another to
merge them can leave the old card highlighted.

Relax GPU Win/9070 XT driver

Removes the explicit driver component of the experimental Win/9070 XT
configuration. The one machine that has a 9070 XT was part of an
experiment that was over and it is currently being used by a Dawn team
member to reproduce specific issues. One issue will require a newer
driver version, so remove the explicit version dimension so that the
machine continues to match.

383ac458818a1ac19c44f361abf8c27805eaebed [ diff ] 
[Composebox] Add SRP input modes for tabs and Drive files

The server will send an SRP input mode that tells Chrome which
attachments the Search results page (SRP) can handle. Besides the four
modes Chrome already routes by (crrev.com/c/8665599), it adds three that
let tabs and Drive files go to SRP, in either order:
any_combination_images_files_tabs, any_combination_images_files_drive
and any_combination_all_types. Chrome adds them now, so it understands
them once the server sends them.

A query goes to SRP only if the mode covers every kind of attachment in
it. For example, an image and a tab go to SRP in
any_combination_images_files_tabs and any_combination_all_types, and to
AI Mode otherwise. The existing modes still send every tab and Drive
file to AI Mode.

Also renames any_combination to any_combination_images_files, so every
name matches the server's. Nothing sets the param yet.

The srp_input_mode param still defaults to single_image, so there's no
behaviour change unless the param is set.

Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter='InputStateModel*'

f29a63d82beeeb79cdb61ac24d1ffb5545ce94a0 [ diff ] 
updater: report bad input and write errors in Mac SetPlatformPolicies()

SetPlatformPolicies() skipped policy values that were not strings,
integers, or booleans without any failure, so a test could believe it
had set a policy that was never written. It also dropped the NSError
from the Managed Preferences write, which made write failures on bots
hard to diagnose.

Add a test failure that names the unsupported value type, policy, and
app, and include the error description in the write failure message.

Add keyboard shortcut to toggle Vertical Tabs collapsed state

Adds the Ctrl + Shift + L keyboard shortcut (KeyEvent.KEYCODE_L with
META_CTRL_ON | META_SHIFT_ON) mapped to a new semantic meaning
TOGGLE_VERTICAL_TABS_COLLAPSE. This allows users on devices with
physical keyboards to quickly expand or collapse the vertical tab strip.

Demo: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDkwMjQ4NjU0MzQzMzcyOHxmYTZjNzliNS04Mw

NO_IFTTT=Appending a KeyboardShortcutsSemanticMeaning value keeps the
existing histogram version, so histograms.xml is unchanged; Ctrl+Shift+L
is handled by the Java KeyboardShortcuts path and the native accelerator
is desktop-only, so BrowserCommandDelegate is unaffected.

7d2b8179545cd2ed230b2ec0b6484227121a057b [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Match AskG side panel description icon to the panel side

The side panel icon in front of "Opens in side panel" always showed the
panel on the left in LTR, and was mirrored in RTL. When the contextual
tasks side panel opens on the right (for example with
ContextualTasksSidePanelDefaultRight, or when the user moves it in
Settings), the icon pointed the wrong way.

WebuiOmniboxHandler now picks the icon from the contextual tasks side
panel alignment, using side_panel_prefs::IsSidePanelRightAligned():
dock_to_left.svg (panel on the right) or dock_to_right.svg (panel on
the left). The alignment pref is the physical side and already accounts
for RTL, so remove the CSS RTL mirror.

The left-aligned icon is unchanged and matches the original mock
(b:571696529). The right-aligned icon is its mirror image.

left side open: http://screenshot/6ct95ejsqijh0
right side open: http://screenshot/2d3ge7sgrqdu8

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter='WebuiOmniboxHandlerTest.*'
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=CrComponentsSearchboxTest.RealboxMatchTest

[Omnibox] Re-disable flaky `OTRRequestIsNotDropped` browser test

https://crrev.com/c/8722418 re-enabled four AIM eligibility browser
tests (`ComprehensiveEligibilityTest`, `RequestWhenOfflineAtStartup`,
`NoRequestOnSubsequentNetworkChanges`, and `OTRRequestIsNotDropped` on
Linux and Windows). While the first three tests remain stable across
gardened builders,
`ChromeAimEligibilityServiceOAuthBrowserTest.OTRRequestIsNotDropped` is
very flaky.

Because the test was already disabled on ChromeOS and macOS, re-disable
`OTRRequestIsNotDropped` unconditionally while keeping the other three
healthy browser tests from https://crrev.com/c/8722418 enabled.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

parent
271e049a2f19acbcaeb9e869a8a644406c972fcd [ diff ] 
updater: write Mac platform policies once in SetPlatformPolicies()

SetPlatformPolicies() rewrote the whole Managed Preferences file on
every iteration of its loop over apps, each time with the policies
collected so far. Build the policy dictionary first and write the file
once.

With an empty `values`, the function now writes an empty policy
dictionary instead of leaving the file unchanged. No test passes an
empty `values`.

4c4a0bcd1a173b2e77ef94bbe9ca19d336995086
parent
6d1a185a53e3bbb52a7759ac736398c618170a26 [ diff ] 
Fix canvas 2D path ops on a canvas with no bitmap

isPointInPath(), isPointInStroke() and the transform mutators bailed out
whenever a context had no paint canvas, so a zero-sized OffscreenCanvas
reported every point as outside its path; the new default-on
Canvas2dPathOpsWithoutBitmap feature keeps them working there and the
upstream nobitmap WPT files are imported with the change.

Update outdated border spacing test for LayoutNG

This test needed updates post-LayoutNG. Before LayoutNG, the border
spacing before a row that doesn't fit in the column was sliced at the
column boundary, with the rest of the spacing carrying over into the
next column. After LayoutNG, the border spacing at breaks between rows
is truncated. The second row's padding is updated so all parts line up
at the expected offsets. This is consistent with and has some overlap
with
external/wpt/css/css-break/table/border-spacing-at-breaks.tentative.html

65e42f28d419b2e6b3c6b4c110874593dddae951

tree
d1afdd09796d9225a6d2829b2cbf0ebf6c43c7bc
parent
fc74db0ab06d0d36bb4eb51c786daf2254251fda [ diff ] 
enterprise_companion: avoid null dereference in ~URLRequestContextGetter

URLRequestContextGetter creates `cert_net_fetcher_` together with the
URL request context, the first time GetURLRequestContext() is called.
Its destructor called `cert_net_fetcher_->Shutdown()` unconditionally,
which dereferences null if the context was never created, for example
when an in-process URL loader factory provider is destroyed without
binding a receiver or a cookie handler. In builds with DCHECKs enabled,
the DCHECK in TransitionalURLLoaderFactoryOwner::Core's destructor
creates the context first and hides the bug.

Only shut down the fetcher if it exists, and add a test that destroys a
provider which never created a network context.

tree
c7b567e039a0670d9c017b34231c1272b000994a
parent
85df22d823d65e00bd267aa3b1adedc086850cbf [ diff ] 
updater: destroy UpdaterUtilApp's service proxy before IPC support

UpdaterUtilApp declared `service_proxy_` before `ipc_support_`. Members
are destroyed in reverse order, so the update service proxy, which owns
Mojo endpoints, was released after ScopedIPCSupport and its IPC thread
were torn down.

Declare `ipc_support_` first so that IPC support is created before and
destroyed after the proxy.

tree
58fb2ac21ffd08abb6f7a35dd4982989d88dc12e
parent
64ef5f806dc53fc43cb831fa9286fc6304d9a031 [ diff ] 
Store OperationTokens on the stack in OperationsControllerTest

OperationsController::OperationToken is marked STACK_ALLOCATED(), so
holding a batch of tokens in a std::vector in OperationsControllerTest
required a STACK_ALLOCATED_IGNORE suppression.

Because the batch size is fixed (100) and OperationToken has no default
constructor, this change replaces the std::vector with a std::array of
std::optional<OperationToken> to keep the tokens on the stack and
remove the suppression.

📋 Metadata
commit
72b1153f8e10fe876849af8f78ef00147e966c2a

author

tree
c489fd65b662ed6a61207b6cfd1109fa2b0a4662
parent
efb3bb6f571fb7089228e7aa8ac6010b11fccdad [ diff ] 
Fix scroll-snap and open offset for tall overscroll areas

When an overscroll area is taller than its container, it has a covering
snap range on the y-axis with center alignment under SnapAxis::kPair.
Previously:
1. FindSnapPosition under SnapAxis::kPair derived the unsearched axis
   using GetSnapSearchResult without intended_position (returning the
   aligned center offset instead of FindCoveringCandidate when the area
   covers the snapport).
2. FindClosestValidAreaInternal evaluated both the covering candidate
   and the center-aligned candidate, allowing the center-aligned snap
   position to win or block valid covering snap positions.
3. Single-axis vertical scrolls inside a tall area under SnapAxis::kPair
   could select another snap area that was not snapped on the unscrolled
   cross axis, causing an unintended horizontal jump that dismissed the
   overscroll area.
4. FindCoveringCandidate treated non-snapping or non-overlapping areas
   as intruders, and ClosestSearchResult preferred must_only_result on
   ties.
5. OverscrollAreaTracker::OpenArea opened to the maximum scroll offset
   when start_y == 0, scrolling to the bottom of a tall area instead of
   keeping y at 0.

This CL fixes covering candidate handling, cross-axis preservation under

cc_unittests and blink_unittests.

SnapAxis: :kPair, and OpenArea offset clamping, and adds unit tests in

tree
2713c42502d69ed51489a09f46887ca3edc39f0e
parent
e1d91c100ccdd9314880ce62357d8690673d1b97 [ diff ] 
Fix storage permission test hangs and eager reply race

The mock ContentSettingsManager runs on the renderer thread, which can
block while waiting for a synchronous AllowStorageAccess response.
Move the mock to a dedicated thread, with atomic counters and ordered
teardown, so it can service requests while the renderer is blocked.

The eager permission test also waits for requests to reach the mock,
rather than for replies to populate the renderer's permission cache.
Flush the Mojo remote before accessing storage and checking IPC counts.
Add histogram assertions to verify that eager checks produce cache hits
and disabled eager checks take the synchronous fallback path.

Verification: All four eager-permission test variants passed in
TryJobs covering configurations represented in the flaky LUCI cluster.
Linux ChromeOS ASan, Android x86 Release, and Android 16 x64 Release.
All 14 ContentSettingsAgentImplBrowserTest variants also passed on
each of those jobs, plus macOS ASan, Linux TSan, Windows Release, and
macOS Release.

Add kOverlayPrestitialAd use counter to UKM

Adds kOverlayPrestitialAd use counter to UKM so overlay prestitial ads
are tracked in UKM. kOverlayPrestitialAd are a specific type of
kOverlayPopupAd that appear while the page is still loading and before
the user has interacted with it, as defined by the Better Ads Standards:
https://www.betterads.org/desktop-prestitial-ad/
https://www.betterads.org/mobile-prestitial-ad/.

185d457ea806f9c07803bc062dbd78a91460a9ed [ diff ] 
Deflake BrowserNavigatorTest.Disposition_NewSplitView_CtrlAltClick

The test simulates a Ctrl+Alt click right after navigating and
injecting a link. If the renderer is still deferring commits or main
frame updates at that point (e.g. paint holding until first contentful
paint), WidgetInputHandlerManager drops the mouse events: the browser
gets a kMainThread/kNotConsumed ack, no DOM event fires, no split view
tab is created, and the test times out in
WebContentsAddedObserver::GetWebContents().

Wait for a submitted frame with WaitForCopyableViewInWebContents()
before clicking.

linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi (UTR, --gtest_repeat, no retries):
  before: 8/20 failed; with --ui-test-action-max-timeout=60000: 3/20
          failed (each at the full 60 s, so not a timeout issue)
  after:  0/40 failed
x64 Wayland (mutter), --test-launcher-jobs=24:
  before: 11/100 failed; after: 0/200 failed in the click (3 runs hit
          an unrelated startup WebUI paint timeout under this load)

Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi
Cq-Include-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:linux-wayland-mutter-rel

Revert "PaintTiming: Add LargestContentfulPaintCalculator::PendingMetricsData"

This reverts commit 47cbda03b42435e51702e1114fa37f61ac6e4c0e.

Reason for revert: CHECK failure. See crbug.com/584594613.

Original change's description:
> PaintTiming: Add LargestContentfulPaintCalculator::PendingMetricsData
>
> This is another step towards creating LCP/ICP entries and metrics data
> at paint time. This CL adds PendingMetricsData, which encapsulates the
> data needed at presentation time. In this CL, we compute this data at
> presentation time, but we'll eventually compute it at paint time and
> propagate to presentation time (via Delegates). Splitting this out will
> isolate behavioral changes in a future patch, as this is a essentially a
> pure refactor**.
>
> **This also fixes a long-standing edge case where in the event of a
> tie between image and text size for the per-frame largest candidate,
> we would use the text, but everywhere else (i.e. metrics) uses the
> image.
>

tree
3a7e90df1aaaf4c82c9d6531b2facafd3e791fdc
parent
baf99e1590e74f648fbd3f5a65c9c9e630f6304d [ diff ] 
gpu: Replace memcmp() of test commands with CommandsMatch()

The client implementation unittests compared the expected commands
against the command buffer using memcmp() on a raw pointer, wrapped in
UNSAFE_TODO. This pattern is emitted ~220 times by the command buffer
code generator, making gles2_implementation_unittest_autogen.h the file
with the most UNSAFE_TODO in Chromium.

Store the commands as a span over the ring buffer and add a
CommandsMatch() helper to the test fixtures, doing a bounds-checked
byte comparison. Update the generator and the hand-written tests to use
it, then regenerate.

No behavior change, test-only.

Declare resource_path.h dependency of grit resource maps in //chrome

The resource map headers generated by these grit targets include
ui/base/webui/resource_path.h. Add //ui/base:webui_resource_path to their
public_deps, as done for the WebUI grit targets in crrev.com/c/8397549
and the iOS ones in crrev.com/c/8675078. //chrome/test/data/webui:resources
is not ChromeOS-only, but has the same issue.

This fixes the resource_path.h errors that "gn check --check-generated"
reports for //chrome on a ChromeOS build.

C++ and Java on Android.

the file directly, providing a dedicated download button on the toolbar

Fix null ProfileManager crash in BrowserProcessImpl::EndSession()
📋 Metadata
commit
48cc9c88c16826522f16a5a11ff357760a6f58f3
[ log ]
author
Amit Dror <amit@island.io>
Wed Nov 11 18:00:27 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 18:00:27 2026
tree
ca01df8b6d1a2a2ce178e52a88dc362fc92d5a13
parent
1bc94cb4c20f59674751b28246586eb11d7d8dc4 [ diff ] 
Fix null ProfileManager crash in BrowserProcessImpl::EndSession()

StartTearDown() destroys the ProfileManager well before the status
tray. On Windows, the tray can still receive WM_ENDSESSION in between
(e.g. OS logoff during a normal quit), and SessionEnding() ->
EndSession() then dereferences the null ProfileManager.

Skip the profile loop when there is no ProfileManager; destroyed
profiles have already scheduled their pref writes.

Also commit Local State unconditionally (except on ChromeOS, as
before). It was only committed when the MetricsService existed, which
teardown destroys first. SessionEnding() terminates the process right
after EndSession(), so without this, earlier Local State commits are
not waited for. The normal path is unchanged.

SessionEnding() intentionally does not return early during shutdown:
logoff can arrive while profiles are alive and still need flushing.

NO_IFTTT=Adds a test inside the block; the EndSession platform set is unchanged.

1bc94cb4c20f59674751b28246586eb11d7d8dc4

author

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 17:59:35 2026
tree
4e0e331b6de1d5e8af7a14f0cbabd583e9a11f77
parent
f153b44a6746b73f68a071d8be5d02e99255d906 [ diff ] 
updater: delete Mac platform policies in the integration test Clean()

On macOS, SetPlatformPolicies() writes the updater's platform policies
to /Library/Managed Preferences, but nothing deleted that file. The
policies outlived the test that set them and applied to every later
test on the same bot. For example, a leftover `TargetChannel = beta`
for test1 made the update check carry `release_channel` and fail the
stricter app request matching in IntegrationTestDeviceManagement tests.
On Windows, Clean() already deletes the policies registry key.

Delete the Managed Preferences file in Clean() for system scope, and
flush the preferences cache afterwards. Do this right after the process
cleanup, so that failures in later cleanup steps do not skip it. Share
the path and cache-flush code with SetPlatformPolicies().

Bots that already have leftover policies clean up on their next system
scope test, since SetUp() calls Clean(). User scope tests also read
these policies but cannot delete them without root, so they rely on a
system scope test running on the same bot first.

[Transition] Close current adaptive UI entries last in destroy()
📋 Metadata
commit
c07f4ea4332e9efc32679914b7f5c7e8fa1ea222
[ log ]
author
Mohamed Adel <adelm@google.com>
Wed Nov 11 17:57:35 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 17:57:35 2026
tree
f7b3d4814b84c9c74dd373451342ff02487e68ea
parent
3cb36db7bbc73aace0055f035b4436bfc27d5d13 [ diff ] 
[Transition] Close current adaptive UI entries last in destroy()

destroy() closed pending entries first so that closing a shown entry
would not let its container present a queued one. A shown entry can be
back in its container's queue, though:
- ModalDialogManager suspends the current dialog when a higher-priority
  one is shown, e.g. a WINDOW entry's app-modal dialog over a TRANSIENT
  entry's tab-modal one.
- BottomSheetController requeues the current sheet when a sheet that can
  supersede it is shown, and can show a newer entry first once that
  sheet closes.
destroy() then closed the shown entries in HashMap order, and closing
the current one first made its container present the requeued entry
again during teardown. Insertion order wouldn't fix this, since a
container can show an older entry over a newer one, so ask the container
instead.

- Adds Presenter.isCurrent(). The sheet coordinator asks the
  BottomSheetController for its current content. ModalDialogManager has
  no such getter outside tests, so the dialog coordinator records
  whether its dialog is the last one the manager created.
- destroy() closes pending entries, then shown entries that aren't
  current, then current ones.
- Adds isCurrent() tests for both coordinators, and a test for hiding
  and showing an entry again from onWillShow, which show() handles but
  no test covered.
- Fixes the AdaptiveUiDialogCoordinator.hide() doc: ModalDialogManager
  can't skip its exit animation.

c82b52a62e6b241c8e92bbfd9fe7272c628f71ec [ diff ] 
Run callback on error in WebUIBrowserPageHandler::GetGuestIdForTabId

When WebUIBrowserPageHandler::GetGuestIdForTabId() is called for a tab
that has already closed, it calls mojo::ReportBadMessage() and returns
early without invoking the GetGuestIdForTabIdCallback. Because
ReportBadMessage() does not close the receiver, dropping the uninvoked
callback triggers a Mojo DCHECK (!connected) during shutdown.

Invoke callback with an empty base::UnguessableToken() before returning
on the error path.

Flaky test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3AWebUIBrowserTest%23ColorProviderKeyIncognito?q=VHASH%3Abdbee181b3e0309b

249adbea33a5c1a69e165bcd4b1caefc128cdc7a [ diff ] 
[TTC] Track whether the user muted TTC's microphone

A live TTC session was reported as muted whenever it wasn't capturing
audio. Sessions go live before capture starts, so the UI showed new
sessions as muted. This removes the need for a specific order on going
live and now only represents the state where the user muted their mic.

SessionControllerImpl now keeps track of whether the user muted the
microphone, and IsMicMuted() reports that. Conversation::IsMicMuted() is
no longer used, so it's removed.

[InfoBars] Launch the Installer Downloader infobar migration by default

The Installer Downloader infobar migration (kMigratedInstallerDownloader
of CentralizedInfoBarFramework) is launched at 100% in Finch with no
holdback, but its default in code is still false. Flip the default to
true so that clients without a Finch seed (first run, seedless or
enterprise clients, or once the study expires) use the centralized
BrowserInfoBarManager path instead of silently falling back to the
legacy per-tab delegate.

InstallerDownloaderController::RegisterInfoBar() is made idempotent: a
test-constructed controller can now coexist with the
GlobalFeatures-owned instance, which has already registered the spec.
The legacy-path unit test fixture explicitly sets the param to false,
and the parameterized interactive tests pin both variants explicitly
instead of relying on the default.

The legacy InstallerDownloaderInfoBarDelegate and its
ConfirmInfoBarCreationAllowlist entry will be removed in a follow-up
once this has baked.

Automated Clean-Up: Remove unused UKM <aggregation> for SiteFamiliarityHeuristicResult

An audit of UkmHistory_{1d,7d} over the past 12 months (b/584304583)
found 0 dashboard or F1 queries for these <aggregation> metrics (all
landed >300 days ago). Removing unused aggregations reduces our UKM
pipeline and storage costs. Note that UKM event-metrics collection and
non-aggregated tables are unaffected; only aggregated UkmHistory
entries are removed, and they will no longer be accessible on the UKM
dashboard. If needed in the future, this change can be reverted.

If this change looks good, please Code-Review+1. If your team
queries these in a private script, which our audit misses, please
comment to keep them; or we will land this cleanup after 5 business
days as UKM OWNERS.

[Android] Remove ShoppingServiceHolder from ShoppingPersistedTabData

Obtain ShoppingService per-tab via
ShoppingServiceFactory.getForProfile(tab.getProfile()) instead of
caching ProfileManager.getLastUsedRegularProfile() in a static holder.

ShoppingServiceHolder was carried over from the earlier
OptimizationGuideBridgeHolder (crrev.com/c/6050389), which needed a
one-time static initializer to call
OptimizationGuideBridge.registerOptimizationTypes(PRICE_TRACKING) on
first use. ShoppingService already registers PRICE_TRACKING in C++ on
Profile creation and caches its JNI bridge in UserData per Profile, so
the static holder is unnecessary and incorrectly pins the service to a
single Profile across the process.

[Wayland] Defer incoming drop until async drag data fetch finishes
📋 Metadata
commit
5dd63bdce2b609de189333126320919ac623592d
[ log ]
author

tree
179c865e20f375d9a91d222c623705206bf0c22b
parent
9201bc0bb37fdead87993a732b12e9286eaaf967 [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Defer incoming drop until async drag data fetch finishes

During an incoming external Wayland drag-and-drop session,
WaylandDataDragController::OnDragEnter() enters State::kFetching and
posts an asynchronous pipe-reading task (followed by portal key
extraction on Linux when portal MIME types are offered). When the user
releases the pointer button before fetching finishes, compositors emit
wl_data_device.drop followed by wl_data_device.leave in the same
protocol flush. Previously, OnDragDrop() immediately forwarded the drop
to the window (which DesktopDragDropClientOzone::OnDragDrop() ignored
because data_to_drop_ was still null) and OnDragLeave() cancelled the
in-flight data fetch, losing the dropped items.

Record pending drop and leave notifications when they arrive in
State::kFetching and deliver them in order from
OnDataFetchingFinished() after OnDragDataAvailable() runs, or abort
them cleanly if the target window is destroyed or a new drag starts.
Also guard OnDataFetched() and OnPortalPathsExtracted() against
cancel_flag so a cancelled portal lookup cannot race with a subsequent
session.

In DesktopDragDropClientOzone, remember when OnDragMotion() arrives
before OnDragDataAvailable() and dispatch delegate_->OnDragUpdated()
once the data arrives so drop targets (including Blink's dragover
handling and Data Leak Prevention's current_drag_update_info_) are
updated before a deferred drop is performed.

with overrides on the corresponding LayoutReplaced subclasses. Assume
true as the default and only implement the cases that need to override
that. Notably:

 * LayoutImage which only enables it for <img>

 * LayoutSVGRoot which only enables it for inline <svg> fragments

 * LayoutCustomScrollbarPart

[Transition] Replace Presentation with AdaptiveUiControllerImpl.Presenter

Adds a package-private AdaptiveUiControllerImpl.Presenter interface
(show() and hide(reason, animate)) implemented by the dialog and sheet
coordinators, so the controller maps each entry straight to its
presenter.

- Removes Presentation and its dialog/sheet branches and assumeNonNull.

  set in the controller.
- AdaptiveUiDialogCoordinator.hide() takes the animate flag and ignores
  it, since ModalDialogManager dismisses synchronously.

No behavior change.

Break touchpad scroll latching on direction change
📋 Metadata

dad92a08cdca7727bb297b48b9d17152c7842fc0
[ log ]
author

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 11 17:41:01 2026
tree
fdb00f32295fa411be07fd60847efc292d7c2f74
parent
df2d9dc68bca8808fd73f19356069986dcbabd4e [ diff ] 
Break touchpad scroll latching on direction change

Touchpad scroll sequences that start with a GestureFlingCancel (e.g.
on Wayland, where each finger-down after a fling start produces one)
use the phase-based TOUCHPAD_SCROLL_IN_PROGRESS path in
MouseWheelPhaseHandler. Unlike the timer-based path used for X11, that
path never broke latching when the scroll direction changed. So when
the user started scrolling an inner scroller toward its edge, the
sequence latched to the unscrollable viewport, and reversing direction
without lifting the fingers scrolled nothing.

Apply the same rule as the timer-based path: when the first scroll
update of the sequence was not consumed and the direction along the
main axis of movement changes, end the current sequence and start a
new one so that the new direction can latch to the inner scroller.

Fixed: 461835112

tree
06837f4cc3cb926ae650d99cd5dc6faace4f2fce
parent
4d8651406e909f14d6bc767c03d847b678b8499f [ diff ] 
[android] Initialize BackgroundThreadPoolFieldTrial before early startup

Early feature list initialization on Android
(https://crrev.com/c/7265587 and https://crrev.com/c/7559571) moved
browser ThreadPool creation and
PartitionAllocSupport::ReconfigureEarlyish() into
JNI_InitializeFeatureList_InitializeFeatureList(). However,
ProcessInitializationHandler.onPostNativeStartup() still appended
--background-thread-pool-field-trial from CachedFlags after native
startup, and BackgroundThreadPoolFieldTrial::Initialize() only ran later
in ContentMainRunnerImpl::Initialize(). On launches that initialize the
feature list early, browser ThreadPool creation and PartitionAlloc lock
configuration read an uninitialized trial state.

In addition, onPostNativeStartup() appended the CachedFlags switch even
when --background-thread-pool-field-trial was already present on the
command line, overwriting explicit command-line overrides.

Apply the CachedFlags switch (when not already present on the command
line) before both early and deferred feature list initialization, and
call BackgroundThreadPoolFieldTrial::Initialize() before creating the
browser ThreadPool in JNI_InitializeFeatureList_InitializeFeatureList().

Bug: 384902323

[🌷] Disable flaky CustomTabActivityTest methods

Four CustomTabActivityTest methods flaked on two or more Android CI
builders over the last 4 days, across phone, tablet, automotive and
desktop emulators. Together they make the class one of the most
frequent sources of chrome_public_test_apk step failures. The failures
are timing races: context menu callbacks that never arrive, and 3s
CriteriaHelper polls that time out.

Disable them everywhere until the owners fix the races. Flakes on the
"Page has hit test data" condition (crbug.com/582838827) are not
covered here.

Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Fandroid%5C%3Achrome_public_test_apk%21junit%3Aorg.chromium.chrome.browser.customtabs%3ACustomTabActivityTest%23testContextMenuPreviewPage
Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665493264695201505

[Read Aloud] Include voice name in voice dialog Play button aria-label

In voice-selection-dialog, every voice row's #preview-icon button

every button the identical accessible name "Play" when visited with a
screen reader's browse/virtual cursor.

[Code Health] Make candidate discovery and expiry search deterministic

Code health candidate and expiry scripts previously depended on working
directory luck, left sibling cleanup grouping to the AI, and ran slow
unscoped code-search counts that timed out on common suffixes or
returned zero uses on tool errors.

Resolving candidate paths from the search root avoids missing Java FQN
targets when invoked outside the repository, and grouping same-file
expired histograms by expiry, owners, and prefix in Python keeps batches
predictable. Switching expired-histogram lookup to a full-name file
search with mirror exclusions and fail-closed error handling avoids
last-segment timeouts while still catching ChromeOS platform2 recorders.

[iOS][AuroraAIM] Update Quick Actions Appearance

This change updates the Quick Actions buttons on the NTP to accommodate
new dynamic layout functionality.

In this change list:

* New strings for title text on the Image Generation and Attach Images
  buttons are added.

* The NewTabPageQuickActionsButtonFactory as a provider of static
  buttons is replaced by convenience initializers on the new
  NTPQuickActionButton class, which provide dynamic buttons. Introducing
  this new class enables the quick action buttons to change their layout
  in place to show/hide titles and resize icons, as required by arms of
  the AimButtonRefactor feature that populate the quick actions row with
  more than two (2) buttons.

* The size of the icon for the image generation button is increased and
  its font weight is decreased.

* The widths of the two (2) trailing buttons when there are three (3)
  quick actions visible are updated to stay at 80pt when their titles
  are not visible (split toolbar layout). The two trailing buttons are
  updated to show their titles and adjust their icon sizes when not in
  split toolbar mode (wide layouts). The limit on the width of the AI
  Mode button in split toolbar mode is removed.

Screencast: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjQwMDk3MzU1NDU4MTUwNHxiNzlmNTU2Yi03OA

Fixed: 582272539, 582338954, 582298032

tree
49facccf55598e414add1ab7a5504d81a312b8ae
parent
2fa8021103219d6bfba2c976f103e82d8a083989 [ diff ] 
Stop and resume updates when connection cost changes

Components that set AllowUpdatesOnMeteredConnections() to false (such as
on-device AI models) were only checked at the start of an update and
continued downloading if the network switched to a metered connection
mid-flight.

CrxUpdateService now observes connection cost changes. When the
connection becomes metered, it cancels the downloads of components that
disallow metered updates. Those updates, and updates the update client
refuses on a metered connection, are retried 5 seconds after the
connection stops being metered, at the priority they were requested
with.

Only Windows, Linux, and Android report connection cost changes; other
platforms keep the previous behavior.

Bug: 554104582, b:316933417
Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=ComponentUpdaterMeteredTest.*

55530f204254e334d8ef165e89ba4446a9babc7e [ diff ] 
Scope AndroidNotificationHandler and DownloadController TabModelList

`TabModelList` is being migrated from unscoped global accessors to
explicit profile-scoped and global APIs to support multi-profile on
Android.

Pass `Profile*` into `AndroidNotificationHandler` (matching desktop's
`SendTabToSelfReceivingUiHandler`) so its `TabModelList` observation and
active-tab lookup are scoped to its owning `Profile`. Scope
`GetCurrentWindow()` in `DownloadController` to the `DownloadItem`'s
`Profile*`.

reports for //ash on a ChromeOS build.

65da5597027d50e442d0ce8e6e75e4ef8a89f473
parent
3d85c9c91fef72fc37e82e367a02d40aba8a61cf [ diff ] 
Migrate icu::Locale::getDefault to GetDefaultIcuLocale in ui

Migrates icu::Locale::getDefault() to
base::i18n::GetDefaultIcuLocale(), which is the source of truth for the
default ICU locale. Where an ICU API requires an icu::Locale, the
LanguageTag is converted with base::i18n::IcuLocaleConverter.

The Windows fallback in l10n_util's GetCandidates() now uses
GetDefaultIcuLocale() directly instead of converting the ICU default
locale back into a LanguageTag.

gfx::TruncateString() now finds the last word boundary before the
truncation point with base::i18n::BreakIterator instead of creating an
ICU word break iterator for the default locale. The result is the same,
except when the truncation point is in the middle of a surrogate pair
(e.g. an emoji) that directly follows a word boundary.
icu::BreakIterator::preceding() skipped that boundary, so the last word
was dropped; now it is kept. A test covers this case.

This prepares for banning icu::Locale::getDefault() outside of
base/i18n in https://crrev.com/c/8786620.

87bc2035522eb5df24a2fb18c92b9f656b535d83

tree
65f782c568ab7139097e6263b2cb6c8991478c61
parent
e59a96e40ec6127b43f27b0dc0d47b0b8409bac7 [ diff ] 
[EVP] Stop preserving ports in the EVT tests

The Email Verification Protocol identifies an issuer by a bare host
name, and these tests can now name it that way: the web tests reach it
through the PAC file they install, so the DNS record, the issuer
metadata and the token's `iss` claim no longer carry a port, and
fedcm-delegation drops FedCmPreservePortsForTesting.

The issuer's FedCM endpoints have to be same-origin with that port-less
identifier, which none of the existing well-known formats serve, so the
delegation tests ask for a new "delegation" format rather than bending
"direct", which the well-known validation test relies on as it is.

The ConnectionAllowlistEmailVerificationTest browser tests now serve the
issuer from a second embedded test server and point its port-less origin
at that server with --host-resolver-rules, so the port-preserving branch
of the DNS record check can go away entirely.

DnsRecordAllowsPortWhenPreservingPorts covered that branch, so it goes
too. DnsRecordRejectsPortWhenPreservingPorts takes its place: with the
flag still enabled, a record naming a port is now rejected like any
other.

setup_evt_endpoints() marks the issuer signed in on its own port-less
origin rather than on the ported one, which the browser never consulted.

#ai-assisted
